Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-111-20260915T135838317624Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The shafts and shafting components, such as the thrust and stern tube bearings, couplings, flanges and sealings, shall be designed to withstand the propeller/ice interaction loads as given in section 6.5. The safety factor must be at least 1.3 against yielding for extreme operational loads, 1.5 for fatigue loads and 1.0 against yielding for the blade failure load.
Normalized requirement:
The shafts and shafting components, such as the thrust and stern tube bearings, couplings, flanges and sealings, shall be designed to withstand the propeller/ice interaction loads as given in section 6.5. The safety factor must be at least 1.3 against yielding for extreme operational loads, 1.5 for fatigue loads and 1.0 against yielding for the blade failure load.
Required inputs: Fex_kN

```turtle
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:extremeIceForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide the externally used extreme ice force in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropulsionLoadCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:loadCase ;
            sh:or (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:shaftLineLoadCaseType ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:extremeOperationalLoadCase ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:componentYieldSafetyFactor ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minInclusive 1.3 ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:shaftLineLoadCaseType ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:fatigueLoadCase ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:shaftLineLoadCaseType ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:bladeFailureLoadCase ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:componentYieldSafetyFactor ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minInclusive 1.0 ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            )
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each propulsion load case must identify an allowed shaft-line load case type and satisfy its applicable readiness check."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropellerShaftLineComponent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:propellerShaftLineComponent
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "When a fatigue load case applies, every linked propeller shaft-line component must have a fatigue safety factor of at least 1.5 with a unitless quantity." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasPropulsionLoadCase ?fatigueCase .
                ?fatigueCase nltl:shaftLineLoadCaseType nltl:fatigueLoadCase .
                $this nltl:hasPropellerShaftLineComponent ?component .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?component nltl:fatigueSafetyFactor ?quantity .
                    ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                              qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value >= 1.5
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-002> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionContractDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction contract date is required to determine whether the 2017 Ice Class Regulations apply."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Ships contracted for construction on or after 1 January 2019 but before 5 July 2021 must identify the 2017 Ice Class Regulations as the applicable edition." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionContractDate ?contractDate .
                FILTER (
                    ?contractDate >= "2019-01-01"^^xsd:date &&
                    ?contractDate < "2021-07-05"^^xsd:date
                )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:applicableIceClassRegulationEdition
                        nltl:iceClassRegulationEdition2017 .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
Category A ship means a ship designed for operation in polar waters in at least medium first-year ice, which may include old ice inclusions.
Normalized requirement:
IF a ship is designed to operate in at least medium first-year ice, including possible old-ice inclusions, THEN ship_category = 'A'.
Required inputs: Ship design ice condition and declared Polar Code category

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO_001_ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceCondition ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceConditionValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide a design ice condition to determine applicability of the category requirement."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "When the design ice condition is at least medium first-year ice with possible old-ice inclusions, the ship category must be Category A." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:designIceCondition nltl:mediumFirstYearIceWithPossibleOldIceInclusions .
                OPTIONAL {
                    $this nltl:shipCategory ?category .
                }
                FILTER (
                    !BOUND(?category)
                    || ?category != nltl:polarShipCategoryA
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
1. The upper edge of the warning triangle must be located vertically above the "ICE" mark, 1,000 mm higher than the Summer Load Line in fresh water but in no case higher than the deck line. The sides of the triangle must be 300 mm in length. 2. The ice class draught mark must be located 540 mm abaft of the centre of the load line ring or 540 mm abaft of the vertical line of the timber load line mark, if applicable. 3. The marks and figures must be cut out of 5 - 8 mm plate and then welded to the ship's side. The marks and figures must be painted in a red or yellow reflecting colour in order to make the marks and figures plainly visible, even in ice conditions. 4. The dimensions of all letters must be the same as those used in the load line mark.
Normalized requirement:
1. The upper edge of the warning triangle must be located vertically above the "ICE" mark, 1,000 mm higher than the Summer Load Line in fresh water but in no case higher than the deck line. The sides of the triangle must be 300 mm in length. 2. The ice class draught mark must be located 540 mm abaft of the centre of the load line ring or 540 mm abaft of the vertical line of the timber load line mark, if applicable. 3. The marks and figures must be cut out of 5 - 8 mm plate and then welded to the ship's side. The marks and figures must be painted in a red or yellow reflecting colour in order to make the marks and figures plainly visible, even in ice conditions. 4. The dimensions of all letters must be the same as those used in the load line mark.

CASE TRF-133-P01
Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: Annex III load-line-ring reference branch.
Source clause: Annex III
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED
RDF path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/evaluation/BEHAVIORAL_RDF_R13/rdf/TRF/TRF-133/TRF-133-P01.ttl
RDF SHA-256: 4e77de4b96f81399baed9e9b62afc8b6b29c4ecacb1eff0be5ecda1480926a40
Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/traficom-batch-j/TRF-133-P01/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasIceClassDraughtMarking ex:iceMark ;
    nltl:hasWarningTriangleMarking ex:triangle ;
    nltl:timberLoadLineMarkApplicable false .

ex:draughtMarkAftOffsetValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 540.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MilliM .

ex:iceMark a nltl:iceClassDraughtMarking ;
    nltl:draughtMarkAftOffset ex:draughtMarkAftOffsetValue ;
    nltl:draughtMarkAftReferencePoint nltl:loadLineRingCentreReference ;
    nltl:letterDimensionsEqualLoadLineMark true ;
    nltl:markingPlainlyVisibleInIceConditions true ;
    nltl:markingPlateThickness ex:markThickness ;
    nltl:markingWeldedToShipSide true ;
    nltl:reflectingMarkingColour nltl:yellowReflectingMarkingColour .

ex:markThickness a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 6.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MilliM .

ex:triThickness a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 6.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MilliM .

ex:triangle a nltl:warningTriangleMarking ;
    nltl:markingAtOrBelowDeckLine true ;
    nltl:markingPlainlyVisibleInIceConditions true ;
    nltl:markingPlateThickness ex:triThickness ;
    nltl:markingWeldedToShipSide true ;
    nltl:reflectingMarkingColour nltl:redReflectingMarkingColour ;
    nltl:verticalOffsetAboveSummerFreshWaterLoadLine ex:verticalOffsetAboveSummerFreshWaterLoadLineValue ;
    nltl:warningTriangleSideLength ex:warningTriangleSideLengthValue ;
    nltl:warningTriangleUpperEdgeVerticallyAboveIceMark true .

ex:verticalOffsetAboveSummerFreshWaterLoadLineValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 1000.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MilliM .

ex:warningTriangleSideLengthValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 300.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:MilliM .


```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF133ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
            nltl:iceClassIi
            nltl:iceClassIii
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must have one permitted Finnish-Swedish ice-class value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIceClassDraughtMarking ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassDraughtMarking ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF133IceClassDraughtMarkingShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must have one conforming ice-class draught marking."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWarningTriangleMarking ;
        sh:class nltl:warningTriangleMarking ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF133WarningTriangleMarkingShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must have one conforming warning triangle marking."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:timberLoadLineMarkApplicable ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasIceClassDraughtMarking ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:draughtMarkAftReferencePoint ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:timberLoadLineVerticalReference
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:timberLoadLineMarkApplicable ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasIceClassDraughtMarking ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:draughtMarkAftReferencePoint ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue nltl:loadLineRingCentreReference
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "The timber-load-line applicability selector and its corresponding draught-mark reference must be explicitly conforming." .

gen:TRF133IceClassDraughtMarkingShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:iceClassDraughtMarking ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:draughtMarkAftOffset ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minInclusive "540"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxInclusive "540"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ice-class draught mark must be 540 mm abaft the applicable reference."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:draughtMarkAftReferencePoint ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:loadLineRingCentreReference
            nltl:timberLoadLineVerticalReference
        )
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:letterDimensionsEqualLoadLineMark ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:markingPlateThickness ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minInclusive "5"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:maxInclusive "8"^^xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The marking plate thickness must be between 5 mm and 8 mm inclusive."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:markingWeldedToShipSide ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:reflectingMarkingColour ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:redReflectingMarkingColour
            nltl:yellowReflectingMarkingColour
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The marking must have one permitted red or yellow reflecting colour."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:markingPlainlyVisibleInIceConditions ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] .

gen:TRF133WarningTriangleMarkingShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:warningTriangleMarking ;
    sh:property [

                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1

            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:MilliM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;

    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalOffsetAboveSummerFreshWaterLoadLine ;

    ] ;
    sh:property [
```

Original source text:
The ship has in operation an approved sewage treatment plant certified by the Administration to meet the operational requirements in either regulation 9.1.1 or 9.2.1 of MARPOL Annex IV, and discharges sewage in accordance with regulation 11.1.2 of Annex IV and shall be as far as practicable from the nearest land, any ice-shelf, fast ice or areas of ice concentration exceeding 1/10.
Normalized requirement:
IF sewage_discharge_uses_treatment_plant = true, THEN sewage_treatment_plant_approval_status = approved, MARPOL_IV_9_1_1_or_9_2_1_certification_status = compliant, MARPOL_IV_11_1_2_discharge_status = compliant, and distances to land, ice shelf, fast ice, and ice concentration greater than 1/10 shall be recorded.
Required inputs: Treatment-plant certificate, MARPOL compliance evidence, and discharge position/ice data

            ]

```turtle
    ] ;

        sh:minCount 1 ;

                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;

                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]

    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:DistanceQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A discharge distance quantity shall have one decimal numeric value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudt:MI_N ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A discharge distance shall use nautical miles."
    ] .

gen:DischargeDistanceRecordShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:dischargeDistanceRecords ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:distanceToNearestLandIceShelfOrFastIce ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node gen:DistanceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The discharge distance record shall record the distance to the nearest land, ice shelf, or fast ice."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:distanceToAreaWithIceConcentrationGreaterThanOneTenth ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node gen:DistanceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The discharge distance record shall record the distance to an area with ice concentration greater than one tenth."
    ] .

gen:IMO117ApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageTreatmentPlantApprovalStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable sewage treatment plant shall have approved status."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation9Point1Point1Or9Point2Point1CertificationStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Applicable MARPOL Annex IV certification status evidence shall be recorded."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation11Point1Point2DischargeComplianceStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Applicable sewage discharge shall comply with MARPOL Annex IV regulation 11.1.2."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolCertificationStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "MARPOL certification status evidence shall be recorded."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasDischargeDistanceRecord ;
        sh:class nltl:dischargeDistanceRecords ;
        sh:node gen:DischargeDistanceRecordShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable ship shall have a discharge distance record."
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:DistanceQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A discharge distance quantity must have one decimal numeric value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MI_N> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A discharge distance must use nautical miles."
    ] .

gen:DischargeDistanceRecordShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:dischargeDistanceRecords ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:distanceToNearestLandIceShelfOrFastIce ;
        sh:node gen:DistanceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each applicable discharge-distance record must record the distance to the nearest land, ice shelf, or fast ice."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:distanceToAreaWithIceConcentrationGreaterThanOneTenth ;
        sh:node gen:DistanceQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each applicable discharge-distance record must record the distance to an area with ice concentration greater than one tenth."
    ] .

gen:ApplicableSewageDischargeShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeUsesTreatmentPlant ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageTreatmentPlantApprovalStatus ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable sewage treatment plant must have approved status."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation9Point1Point1Or9Point2Point1CertificationStatus ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The MARPOL Annex IV regulation 9.1.1 or 9.2.1 certification status must be approved."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation11Point1Point2DischargeComplianceStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Discharge must comply with MARPOL Annex IV regulation 11.1.2."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolCertificationStatus ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "MARPOL certification status must be approved."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:marpolDischargeStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "MARPOL discharge status must indicate compliance."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasDischargeDistanceRecord ;
        sh:node gen:DischargeDistanceRecordShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable sewage discharge must have a discharge-distance record."
    ] .

gen:NonApplicableSewageDischargeShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeUsesTreatmentPlant ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:IMO117ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeUsesTreatmentPlant ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The sewage treatment plant applicability value must be recorded as a single boolean."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:ApplicableSewageDischargeShape
        gen:NonApplicableSewageDischargeShape
    ) .

```

        [

```turtle
                sh:path nltl:headingOrPositionSystem ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "When a heading-reference or position-fixing system is applicable, it must be identified."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:intendedArea ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Each applicable heading-reference or position-fixing system must have an intended area."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:intendedAreaSuitabilityApprovalStatus ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:in ( nltl:evidenceStateApproved ) ;
                sh:message "The intended-area suitability approval status for each applicable heading-reference or position-fixing system must be Approved."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:headingOrPositionSystem ;
                sh:maxCount 0 ;
                sh:message "No heading-reference or position-fixing system is present."
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Original source text:
Ships of 500 gross tonnage and upwards shall have two non-magnetic means to determine and display their heading. Both means shall be independent and shall be connected to the ship's main and emergency source of power.
Normalized requirement:
IF gross_tonnage >= 500, THEN non_magnetic_heading_means_count >= 2; the means shall be independent and each connected to main_power and emergency_power.
Required inputs: Gross tonnage, heading-equipment inventory, independence relation, and power connections

```turtle
<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO26-012> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:not [
                sh:property [
                    sh:path nltl:grossTonnage ;
                    sh:minCount 1 ;
                    sh:node [
                        sh:property [
                            sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                            sh:minCount 1 ;
                            sh:minInclusive 500 ;
                            sh:datatype xsd:decimal
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:grossTonnage ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:minInclusive 500 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
                sh:minCount 2 ;
                sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:connectedToMainPower ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:grossTonnage ?gross .
                ?gross qudt:numericValue ?value .
                FILTER (xsd:decimal(?value) >= 500)
                $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?means .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?means a nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
                        nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ?peer .
                    FILTER (?peer != ?means)
                    ?peer a nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans .
                    $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?peer .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gs:ShipHeadingMeansShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
        sh:node gs:NonMagneticHeadingMeansShape ;
        sh:message "Represented non-magnetic heading means must satisfy the required topology constraints."
    ] .

gs:NonMagneticHeadingMeansShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectedToMainPower ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each represented non-magnetic heading means must be connected to main power."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each represented non-magnetic heading means must be connected to emergency power."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each represented non-magnetic heading means must identify an independent heading means."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "An independence relation must point to a distinct non-magnetic heading means linked to the same ship." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?means .
                ?means nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ?other .
                FILTER (
                    ?means = ?other ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?other .
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:connectedToMainPower ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means must be connected to main power."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means must be connected to emergency power."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means must identify an independent heading means."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means must be independent from another heading means belonging to the same ship." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?ship nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans $this .
                            $this nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ?other .
                            FILTER (?other != $this)
                            ?ship nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?other .
                        }
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ] .

```

Clause / section / page: 6.5.1 / Design loads on propeller blades / 32
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-075-20260915T133438519183Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Fb is the maximum force experienced during the lifetime of a ship that bends a propeller blade backwards when the propeller mills an ice block while rotating ahead. Ff is the maximum force experienced during the lifetime of a ship that bends a propeller blade forwards when the propeller mills an ice block while rotating ahead. Fb and Ff originate from different propeller/ice interaction phenomena, and do not occur simultaneously. Hence, they are to be applied to one blade separately.
Normalized requirement:
Fb is the maximum force experienced during the lifetime of a ship that bends a propeller blade backwards when the propeller mills an ice block while rotating ahead. Ff is the maximum force experienced during the lifetime of a ship that bends a propeller blade forwards when the propeller mills an ice block while rotating ahead. Fb and Ff originate from different propeller/ice interaction phenomena, and do not occur simultaneously. Hence, they are to be applied to one blade separately.

```turtle
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseIceBlock ;

            sh:maxCount 1 ;
            sh:node gen:TRF075ForceShape
        ]
    ) ;
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF075ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ;
        sh:node gen:TRF075LoadCaseShape
    ] .

gen:TRF075LoadCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:propellerBladeLoadCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseBlade ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:propellerBlade ;
        sh:message "Each propeller blade load case must link to exactly one propeller blade."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseIceBlock ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:iceBlock ;
        sh:message "Each propeller blade load case must link to exactly one ice block."
    ] ;
    sh:xone (
        [
            sh:path nltl:backwardBladeForce ;
            sh:minCount 1 ;
            sh:maxCount 1 ;
            sh:node gen:TRF075ForceQuantityShape
        ]
        [
            sh:path nltl:forwardBladeForce ;
            sh:minCount 1 ;
            sh:maxCount 1 ;
            sh:node gen:TRF075ForceQuantityShape
        ]
    ) .

gen:TRF075ForceQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue unit:KiloN
    ] .

```

Original source text:
Scantlings of category A ships shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account standards acceptable to the Organization or other standards offering an equivalent level of safety; scantlings of category B ships shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account standards acceptable to the Organization or other standards offering an equivalent level of safety; and scantlings of ice strengthened category C ships shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account acceptable standards adequate for the ice types and concentrations encountered in the area of operation.
Normalized requirement:
For category A, category B, and ice-strengthened category C ships, scantling_approval_status shall be approved by the Administration or an accepted recognized organization; the approval standard shall be accepted or provide equivalent safety, and category C approval shall address operating-area ice type and concentration.
Required inputs: Ship category, ice-strengthening status, scantling approval, and standard evidence

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO032ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO032CategoryABBranch
        gen:IMO032CategoryCStrengthenedBranch
        gen:IMO032CategoryCNotStrengthenedBranch
    ) .

gen:IMO032CategoryABBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarShipCategoryA
            nltl:polarShipCategoryB
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A category A or B ship shall identify its ship category."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasApprovalRecord ;
        sh:class nltl:approvalRecord ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:IMO032ApprovalABShape ;
        sh:message "A category A or B ship shall have an approved scantling approval record."
    ] .

gen:IMO032CategoryCStrengthenedBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryC ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The applicable ship category shall be category C."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "An applicable category C ship shall identify that it is ice strengthened."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasApprovalRecord ;
        sh:class nltl:approvalRecord ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:IMO032ApprovalCShape ;
        sh:message "An ice-strengthened category C ship shall have an approval record covering its operating ice conditions."
    ] .

gen:IMO032CategoryCNotStrengthenedBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryC ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
    ] .

gen:IMO032ApprovalABShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:scantlingApprovalStatus ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The scantling approval status shall be approved."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvingAuthority ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "Administration"
            "recognized organization accepted by the Administration"
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The scantling approval shall be by the Administration or an accepted recognized organization."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvalStandard ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "standard acceptable to the Organization"
            "another standard offering an equivalent level of safety"
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The approval standard shall be acceptable to the Organization or offer an equivalent level of safety."
    ] .

gen:IMO032ApprovalCShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:scantlingApprovalStatus ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The scantling approval status shall be approved."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvingAuthority ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "Administration"
            "recognized organization accepted by the Administration"
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The scantling approval shall be by the Administration or an accepted recognized organization."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvalStandard ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "acceptable standard adequate for operating ice type and concentration"
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The approval standard shall be adequate for the operating ice type and concentration."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingIceType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The category C approval record shall specify the operating ice type."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingIceConcentration ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:IMO032IceConcentrationQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "The category C approval record shall specify the operating ice concentration as a unitless quantity."
    ] .

gen:IMO032IceConcentrationQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Operating ice concentration shall have a decimal numeric value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Operating ice concentration shall use the unitless unit."
    ] .
```

```turtle
            nltl:polarShipCategoryC
        ) ;
        sh:message "The ship category must be recorded as Category A, B, or C."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "The ice-strengthening status must be recorded."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gs:IMO032CategoryAShape
        gs:IMO032CategoryBShape
        gs:IMO032StrengthenedCategoryCShape
        gs:IMO032UnstrengthenedCategoryCShape
    ) .

gs:IMO032CategoryAShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryA
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasApprovalRecord ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:IMO032ApprovalABShape ;
        sh:message "A Category A ship must have a conforming scantling approval record."
    ] .

gs:IMO032CategoryBShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryB
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasApprovalRecord ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:IMO032ApprovalABShape ;
        sh:message "A Category B ship must have a conforming scantling approval record."
    ] .

gs:IMO032StrengthenedCategoryCShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryC
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasApprovalRecord ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:IMO032ApprovalStrengthenedCShape ;
        sh:message "An ice-strengthened Category C ship must have an approval record covering its operating ice conditions."
    ] .

gs:IMO032UnstrengthenedCategoryCShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:polarShipCategoryC
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipIceStrengthened ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] .

gs:IMO032ApprovalCommonShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:scantlingApprovalStatus ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:message "The scantling approval status must be Approved."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvingAuthority ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "Administration"
            "recognized organization accepted by the Administration"
        ) ;
        sh:message "The approval must be by the Administration or a recognized organization accepted by the Administration."
    ] .

gs:IMO032ApprovalABShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node gs:IMO032ApprovalCommonShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvalStandard ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "standard acceptable to the Organization"
            "another standard offering an equivalent level of safety"
        ) ;
        sh:message "Category A or B approval must use an Organization-acceptable standard or an equivalent-safety standard."
    ] .

gs:IMO032ApprovalStrengthenedCShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:node gs:IMO032ApprovalCommonShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvalStandard ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:in (
            "acceptable standard adequate for operating ice type and concentration"
        ) ;
        sh:message "Ice-strengthened Category C approval must use an acceptable standard adequate for the operating ice type and concentration."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingIceType ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:message "The operating ice type must be recorded for ice-strengthened Category C approval."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:operatingIceConcentration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:IMO032IceConcentrationShape ;
        sh:message "The operating ice concentration must be recorded for ice-strengthened Category C approval."
    ] .

gs:IMO032IceConcentrationShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue qudt:UNITLESS
    ] .
```

Clause / section / page: 1.2.1 / Part I-A Chapter 1 - Definitions / 13

Original source text:
Bergy waters mean an area of freely navigable water in which ice of land origin is present in concentrations less than 1/10. There may be sea ice present, although the total concentration of all ice shall not exceed 1/10.
Normalized requirement:
IF ice_condition = 'bergy waters', THEN land_origin_ice_concentration_tenths < 1 AND total_ice_concentration_tenths <= 1.
Required inputs: Land-origin and total ice concentrations

```turtle
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

SELECT $this

}
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_009_BergyWatersShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceCondition ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:message "A ship must specify exactly one ice condition." ;
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceCondition ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue "bergy waters"^^xsd:string ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:landOriginIceConcentration ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxExclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                    ] ;
                ] ;
                sh:message "For bergy waters, land-origin ice concentration must be less than 1/10." ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:totalIceConcentration ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "1"^^xsd:decimal ;
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                    ] ;
                ] ;
                sh:message "For bergy waters, total ice concentration must not exceed 1/10." ;
            ] ;
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The non-bergy-waters applicability branch cannot be used when the ice condition is bergy waters." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:iceCondition ?condition .
                        FILTER (?condition = "bergy waters"^^xsd:string)
                    }
                """ ;
            ] ;
        ]
    ) .

```
